## Corpus Check
- 1621 files · ~2,346,629 words

## Summary
- 34570 nodes · 70575 edges · 1368 communities (1181 shown, 187 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6937 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `3addbe90`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
